--- .env.example ---
# Server-only secrets. Set these as Cloudflare Worker secrets in production;
# never commit real values. NEXT_PUBLIC_* values are compiled into the browser
# bundle at build time, so changing one requires a rebuild and redeploy.

# --- Optional: model-written research assistant and author avatars ---------
# Without GEMINI_API_KEY the assistant returns a deterministic, cited answer
# assembled from the local catalogue.
GEMINI_API_KEY=
# Defaults to gemini-2.5-flash when unset.
GEMINI_MODEL=
# Optional Cloudflare AI Gateway base URL for routing Gemini traffic.
AI_GATEWAY_BASE_URL=

# --- Metered Assistant API v1 (/api/v1/* and MCP ask_archive) --------------
# Signs privileged API-key auth & quota ledger RPCs (`authenticate_api_key`,
# `reserve_api_quota`, `finalize_api_usage`, `refund_api_usage`). Must match
# the row inserted into `private.api_settings`.
API_BRIDGE_SECRET=
# Optional override for free monthly API quota (default 50).
API_FREE_MONTHLY_REQUESTS=

# --- Optional: Web Bot Auth identity for outbound bot GET/HEAD requests ---
# Store a private Ed25519 JWK (JSON containing x and d) as a runtime secret.
# Do not set this as NEXT_PUBLIC_* or commit it. Without it, the public
# signature directory returns 503 rather than advertising an unverifiable key.
WEB_BOT_AUTH_PRIVATE_JWK=

# --- Optional: transactional email (Resend) --------------------------------
# Without this every send is skipped rather than failing. A runtime secret, so
# in production use `wrangler secret put RESEND_API_KEY`, not a build variable.
RESEND_API_KEY=
# Optional inbound webhook signing secret and local testing override.
RESEND_WEBHOOK_SECRET=
EMAIL_ALLOW_LOCAL_TEST=
# Optional uploaded Resend template IDs per scenario. Populate with
# `npm run email:template:sync`. See docs/email-scenarios.md.
RESEND_TPL_WELCOME=
RESEND_TPL_VERIFY_EMAIL=
RESEND_TPL_PASSWORD_RESET=
RESEND_TPL_SECURITY_ALERT=
RESEND_TPL_SUPPORT_REPLY=

# --- Public, build-time values ---------------------------------------------
# Canonical origin for metadata, sitemap, robots, and same-origin checks.
# Defaults to https://researchwith.me.
NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL=
# Project URL and publishable key. Both enable the Supabase CMS, auth,
# storage, and realtime; without them the app runs in local demo mode.
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=

# --- Optional: cache-eviction webhook --------------------------------------
# Shared secret for POST /api/revalidate, sent as the x-revalidate-secret
# header. The legacy name MY_SECRET_TOKEN is still accepted.
REVALIDATE_SECRET=

# --- Lemon Squeezy billing -----------------------------------------------
# Lemon Squeezy is the merchant of record: it charges the card, pays the tax,
# and emails the receipt. Reading on this site is never gated by any of this.
# `docs/billing.md` is the runbook; this is only the key inventory.
#
# Secrets. Set with `npx wrangler secret put <NAME>` — never in vars, never in
# a committed file. All three stay empty here.
LEMONSQUEEZY_API_KEY=
LEMONSQUEEZY_WEBHOOK_SECRET=
# Signs the privileged write into the database. The SAME value must also be
# inserted into the `private.billing_settings` row by the migration's owner
# step, or every webhook will be rejected with billing_bridge_unauthorized.
# Rotate in that order: Worker secret first, then the table row.
BILLING_BRIDGE_SECRET=
#
# Runtime vars (`vars` in wrangler.jsonc, or the deployment dashboard).
# IDs come from the Lemon Squeezy dashboard, per environment.
LEMONSQUEEZY_STORE_ID=
LEMONSQUEEZY_VARIANT_TEARDOWN=
LEMONSQUEEZY_VARIANT_STAT_TABLES=
# Upcoming ResearchWithMe Co-pilot subscription variant ID.
LEMONSQUEEZY_VARIANT_COPILOT=
# `1` in previews so nothing can charge a real card; unset means production.
LEMONSQUEEZY_TEST_MODE=
